Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on bosons structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 517. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 905 with relations in degree 5.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^905(S, F) is trivial. QED.
